{
  "content": "\"\"\"Flatten gf180mcuD ngspice model-card parameter expressions to literals.\n\nOnly .model blocks are rewritten. Parameters whose values depend on\ntemper/dtemp/agauss/mc_ are left untouched (PVT/statistical correctness).\n\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sys\n\nSRC = sys.argv[1]\nDST = sys.argv[2]\n\ntext = open(SRC).read()\nlines = text.splitlines()\n\n# collect global .param definitions (last wins, like ngspice)\nparams = {}\nfor ln in lines:\n    s = ln.strip()\n    m = re.match(r\"\\.param\\s+(.+)\", s, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if m:\n        for kv in re.findall(r\"(\\w+)\\s*=\\s*('[^']*'|[^\\s]+)\", m.group(1)):\n            params[kv[0]] = kv[1]\n\nDYNAMIC = re.compile(r\"temper|dtemp|agauss|gauss|\\bmc_|\\brandom|\\bmin\\b|\\bmax\\b\", re.I)\n\n\ndef resolve(tok, depth=0):\n    \"\"\"Return a float literal string for tok, or None if not constant.\"\"\"\n    if depth > 12:\n        return None\n    tok = tok.strip()\n    if tok.startswith(\"'\") and tok.endswith(\"'\"):\n        tok = tok[1:-1].strip()\n        # substitute every identifier inside the expression\n        def sub(mm):\n            r = resolve(params.get(mm.group(0), mm.group(0)), depth + 1)\n            return r if r is not None else mm.group(0)\n        expr = re.sub(r\"[A-Za-z_]\\w*\", sub, tok)\n        if DYNAMIC.search(expr) or re.search(r\"[A-Za-z_]\", expr):\n            return None\n        try:\n            return repr(float(eval(expr, {\"__builtins__\": {}}, {})))\n        except Exception:\n            return None\n    try:\n        return repr(float(tok))\n    except ValueError:\n        return None\n\n\nresolved = {k: v for k, v in ((k, resolve(v)) for k, v in params.items())\n            if v is not None}\nprint(f\"params total={len(params)} flattened={len(resolved)}\")\n\nQREF = re.compile(r\"'([^']+)'\")\nout, in_model, skipped = [], False, 0\n\n\ndef flat_expr(expr):\n    def sub(mm):\n        return resolved.get(mm.group(0), mm.group(0))\n    e = re.sub(r\"[A-Za-z_]\\w*\", sub, expr)\n    if re.search(r\"[A-Za-z_]\", e):          # something unresolved left\n        return None\n    try:\n        return repr(float(eval(e, {\"__builtins__\": {}}, {})))\n    except Exception:\n        return None\n\n\nfor ln in lines:\n    s = ln.strip()\n    low = s.lower()\n    if low.startswith(\".model\"):\n        out.append(ln)\n        in_model = True\n        continue\n    if in_model:\n        if s.startswith(\"+\") or (s and not low.startswith((\".end\", \".model\")) and \"=\" in s and s.startswith(\"+\")):\n            def repl(mm):\n                global skipped\n                f = flat_expr(mm.group(1))\n                if f is None:\n                    skipped += 1\n                    return mm.group(0)\n                return f\n            out.append(QREF.sub(repl, ln))\n            continue\n        in_model = False\n        out.append(ln)\n        continue\n    out.append(ln)\n\nopen(DST, \"w\").write(\"\\n\".join(out) + \"\\n\")\nprint(f\"left dynamic/unresolvable refs inside models: {skipped}\")\nprint(\"wrote\", DST)\n",
  "filePath": "/tmp/opencode/build/flatten.py"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"Flatten gf180mcuD ngspice model cards to literal parameters (nominal corner).\n\nBinds design.ngspice default switches (sw_stat_global=0, sw_stat_mismatch=0,\nfnoicor=0, mc_skew/res_mc_skew/cap_mc_skew=3); statistical terms multiply\nthose zeros and fold away. Expressions containing temper/dtemp stay dynamic.\nCorrectness is verified separately by comparing operating points.\n\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sys\n\nSRC_LIB, SRC_DESIGN, DST = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]\n\nlines = open(SRC_LIB).read().splitlines()\nparams = {}\nfor ln in open(SRC_DESIGN):\n    s = ln.strip()\n    m = re.match(r\"\\.param\\s+(.+)\", s, re.I)\n    if m:\n        for k, v in re.findall(r\"(\\w+)\\s*=\\s*('[^']*'|[^\\s]+)\", m.group(1)):\n            params[k] = v\nfor ln in lines:\n    s = ln.strip()\n    m = re.match(r\"\\.param\\s+(.+)\", s, re.I)\n    if m:\n        for k, v in re.findall(r\"(\\w+)\\s*=\\s*('[^']*'|[^\\s]+)\", m.group(1)):\n            params[k] = v\n\nBIND = {}\nDYNAMIC = re.compile(r\"temper|dtemp\", re.I)\nZEROFOLD = re.compile(r\"agauss|gauss|random|\\bmc_\", re.I)\n\n\ndef ev(expr):\n    env = {\"__builtins__\": {}}\n    return float(eval(expr, env, BIND))\n\n\ndef resolve(tok, depth=0):\n    if depth > 16:\n        return None\n    tok = tok.strip()\n    if tok.startswith(\"'\") and tok.endswith(\"'\"):\n        tok = tok[1:-1].strip()\n        if DYNAMIC.search(tok):\n            return None\n\n        def sub(mm):\n            name = mm.group(0)\n            r = resolve(params.get(name, name), depth + 1)\n            return r if r is not None else name\n        e = re.sub(r\"[A-Za-z_]\\w*\", sub, tok)\n        if ZEROFOLD.search(e):\n            # statistical term folded to zero by sw_stat_* == 0\n            try:\n                e = ZEROFOLD.sub(\"0.0\", e)\n                e = re.sub(r\"[A-Za-z_]\\w*\", \"0.0\", e)\n            except Exception:\n                return None\n        if re.search(r\"[A-Za-z_]\", e):\n            return None\n        try:\n            return repr(ev(e))\n        except Exception:\n            return None\n    try:\n        return repr(float(tok))\n    except ValueError:\n        return None\n\n\nresolved = {k: v for k, v in ((k, resolve(v)) for k, v in list(params.items()))\n            if v is not None}\nBIND.update({k: float(v) for k, v in resolved.items()})\nprint(f\"params={len(params)} flattened={len(resolved)}\")\n\nQREF = re.compile(r\"'([^']+)'\")\nout, in_model, kept, flatn = [], False, 0, 0\n\n\ndef repl(mm):\n    global kept, flatn\n    e = mm.group(1)\n    if DYNAMIC.search(e):\n        kept += 1\n        return mm.group(0)\n\n    def sub(m2):\n        return resolved.get(m2.group(0), m2.group(0))\n    e2 = re.sub(r\"[A-Za-z_]\\w*\", sub, e)\n    if ZEROFOLD.search(e2):\n        e2 = ZEROFOLD.sub(\"0.0\", e2)\n        e2 = re.sub(r\"[A-Za-z_]\\w*\", \"0.0\", e2)\n    if re.search(r\"[A-Za-z_]\", e2):\n        kept += 1\n        return mm.group(0)\n    try:\n        f = repr(ev(e2))\n    except Exception:\n        kept += 1\n        return mm.group(0)\n    flatn += 1\n    return f\n\n\nfor ln in lines:\n    s = ln.strip()\n    if s.lower().startswith(\".model\"):\n        out.append(ln)\n        in_model = True\n        continue\n    if in_model:\n        if s.startswith(\"+\"):\n            out.append(QREF.sub(repl, ln))\n            continue\n        in_model = False\n        out.append(ln)\n        continue\n    out.append(ln)\n\nopen(DST, \"w\").write(\"\\n\".join(out) + \"\\n\")\nprint(f\"in-model refs flattened={flatn} left-dynamic={kept}\")\nprint(\"wrote\", DST)\n",
  "filePath": "/tmp/opencode/build/flatten2.py"
}

## Assistant (Build · Ox Alpha Free (Unlimited) · 19.9s)

_Thinking:_

The measure() hung — my all_cross() with `t = tc + 1e-12` loop is O(n²) over 30k points (each cross() rescans from beginning!). Fix: single-pass crossing detection. Rewrite measurement to a fast single-pass implementation.

My crossing finder is O(n²) on 30k points. Rewriting the measurement single-pass:

**Tool: write**

**Input:**
```json
{
  "content": "\"\"\"Fast single-pass oscillator waveform metrics.\"\"\"\nimport sys\n\n\ndef load_dat(path):\n    ts, vs, isup = [], [], []\n    for ln in open(path):\n        p = ln.split()\n        if len(p) < 4 or ln[0] in \"#*@\":\n            continue\n        try:\n            ts.append(float(p[0])); vs.append(float(p[1]))\n            isup.append(float(p[3]))\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n    return ts, vs, isup\n\n\ndef interp(x0, y0, x1, y1, y):\n    return x0 + (y - y0) * (x1 - x0) / (y1 - y0)\n\n\ndef measure(ts, vs, isup, settle_frac=0.25):\n    n = len(ts)\n    i0 = int(n * 0.6)\n    hi, lo = max(vs[i0:]), min(vs[i0:])\n    mid, amp = (hi + lo) / 2.0, (hi - lo) / 2.0\n    if amp < 0.5:\n        return None\n\n    # startup: first time |v-mid| >= 80% final amplitude\n    t_start = None\n    for i in range(n):\n        if abs(vs[i] - mid) >= 0.8 * amp:\n            t_start = ts[i]\n            break\n    if t_start is None:\n        return None\n\n    # steady window: after startup + 3 ns\n    j = 0\n    while j < n and ts[j] < t_start + 3e-9:\n        j += 1\n\n    rises, falls = [], []\n    prev_above = vs[j] >= mid\n    for k in range(j + 1, n):\n        above = vs[k] >= mid\n        if above != prev_above:\n            tc = interp(ts[k - 1], vs[k - 1], ts[k], vs[k], mid)\n            (rises if above else falls).append(tc)\n        prev_above = above\n\n    if len(rises) < 3:\n        return None\n    freq = (len(rises) - 1) / (rises[-1] - rises[0])\n    duties, fi = [], 0\n    for r0, r1 in zip(rises, rises[1:]):\n        while fi < len(falls) and falls[fi] < r0:\n            fi += 1\n        if fi < len(falls) and falls[fi] < r1:\n            duties.append((falls[fi] - r0) / (r1 - r0))\n    duty = sum(duties) / len(duties) * 100.0 if duties else float(\"nan\")\n\n    w0 = next(i for i in range(n) if ts[i] >= rises[0])\n    idx = range(w0, n)\n    voh = max(vs[i] for i in idx)\n    vol = min(vs[i] for i in idx)\n    i_avg = sum(abs(isup[i]) for i in idx) / len(idx)\n    return dict(startup_s=t_start, freq_hz=freq, duty_pct=duty,\n                voh=voh, vol=vol, i_avg=i_avg, cycles=len(rises) - 1)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    ts, vs, isup = load_dat(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else \"osc.dat\")\n    m = measure(ts, vs, isup)\n    if m:\n        print(f\"f={m['freq_hz']/1e6:.3f}MHz duty={m['duty_pct']:.2f}% \"\n              f\"tstart={m['startup_s']*1e9:.0f}ns VOH={m['voh']:.4f} \"\n              f\"VOL={m['vol']:.4f} Iavg={m['i_avg']*1e6:.1f}uA cycles={m['cycles']}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"NO OSCILLATION\")\n",
  "filePath": "/tmp/opencode/osc/omeasure.py"
}
```

* Self-starting 9-stage CMOS ring oscillator
.lib "{PDK_LIB}" typical
.subckt oscillator VDD VSS OSC_OUT
XM1p n1 OSC_OUT VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=2.2u W=3.8u nf=1
XM1n n1 OSC_OUT VSS VSS nfet_03v3 L=2.2u W=2u nf=1

XM2n n2 n1 VSS VSS nfet_03v3 L=2.2u W=2u nf=1
... stages 3..8 ...
XM9p OSC_OUT n8 VDD VDD pfet_03v3 L=2.2u W=4u nf=1
XM9n OSC_OUT n8 VSS VSS nfet_03v3 L=2.2u W=2u nf=1
.ends
```

700:def run_flow(design: DesignPoint, hooks: FlowHooks,
    def run(self, design: DesignPoint) -> FlowResult:
        started = time.time()
        res = FlowResult(cell=design.cell)

        if not self.cfg.specs:
            res.status = Outcome.ERROR
            res.failure = Failure.CONFIGURATION_FAILURE
            res.message = ("no target specifications were given, so there is "
                           "nothing to optimise against. Pass FlowConfig("
                           "specs=[Spec(...), ...]).")
            res.elapsed = time.time() - started
            return res

        design = self._loop_pre_layout(design, res)
        if res.status in (Outcome.ERROR, Outcome.TIMEOUT):
            res.elapsed = time.time() - started
            self._finish(res)
            return res
        if res.failure == Failure.DESIGN_FAILURE and not (
                res.best_pre and res.best_pre.passed):
            # Pre-layout never converged. Going to layout would burn EDA time
            # on a circuit already known to miss its targets.
            res.status = Outcome.NOT_CONVERGED
            res.stage = Stage.PRE_OPTIMIZATION
            res.elapsed = time.time() - started
            self._finish(res)
            return res

        if self.cfg.skip_layout:
            res.stage = Stage.PRE_EVALUATION
            res.status = Outcome.PASS if (res.best_pre and res.best_pre.passed) \
                else Outcome.FAIL
            res.message = "skip_layout=True: stopped after pre-layout evaluation"
            res.elapsed = time.time() - started
            self._finish(res)
            return res

        self._loop_pex(design, res)
        res.elapsed = time.time() - started
        self._finish(res)

```

{
  "content": "\"\"\"Oscillator measurement + tuning library for the MBG full-auto flow.\n\nsimulate_osc_netlist() replaces mbg.flow_runtime.simulate_netlist so both\npre-layout and PEX simulations measure oscillator metrics (frequency, duty\ncycle, swing levels, startup time, average supply current, sustained cycles)\ninstead of the bundled small-signal AC metrics.\n\nSimulation model notes:\n- The GF180MCU ngspice model file ships ~1000 quoted parameter expressions\n  inside .model cards that ngspice re-interprets at every Newton iteration;\n  a 9-stage transient then takes hours instead of seconds. pdk_flat/ holds\n  sm141064.ngspice with those expressions pre-evaluated to the same IEEE\n  doubles (statistical terms fold to their exact zero under the nominal\n  sw_stat_global=0 / sw_stat_mismatch=0 switches). Equivalence was verified:\n  operating points match to all printed digits and a 20 ns device transient\n  is bit-identical to the unflattened library.\n- Supply is an ideal DC source tied at t=0 (normal power-up). There is no\n  stimulus on any signal node and no .ic/.nodeset anywhere: startup comes\n  from the circuit's own stage-1 asymmetry.\n- OSC_OUT sees a 5 fF probe capacitance.\n\"\"\"\nimport math\nimport os\nimport re\n\n_HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nFLAT_LIB = os.path.join(_HERE, \"pdk_flat\", \"sm141064.ngspice\")\n\n\ndef _pdk_design():\n    root = os.environ.get(\"PDKPATH\") or os.path.join(\n        os.environ.get(\"PDK_ROOT\", os.path.expanduser(\"~/.volare\")),\n        os.environ.get(\"PDK\", \"gf180mcuD\"))\n    return os.path.join(root, \"libs.tech\", \"ngspice\", \"design.ngspice\")\n\n\ndef build_deck(core_netlist: str, cell: str, *, vdd=3.3, temp=27,\n               corner=\"typical\", stop=\"12.5u\", step=\"100p\",\n               cprobe=\"5f\") -> str:\n    keep = [l for l in core_netlist.splitlines()\n            if not l.strip().lower().startswith((\".lib\", \".include\", \".temp\"))]\n    head = []\n    design = _pdk_design()\n    if os.path.isfile(design):\n        head.append(f\".include '{design}'\")\n    head.append(f\".lib '{FLAT_LIB}' {corner}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(\n        head + [f\".temp {temp}\", \"\", \"\\n\".join(keep).strip(), \"\",\n                \"Vsfix VSS 0 0\",\n                f\"Vsup VDD 0 {vdd}\",\n                f\"Cprobe OSC_OUT 0 {cprobe}\",\n                f\"Xdut VDD VSS OSC_OUT {cell}\", \"\",\n                \".control\",\n                f\"tran {step} {stop}\",\n                \"wrdata osc.dat v(OSC_OUT) i(Vsup)\",\n                \".endc\", \".end\", \"\"])\n\n\n# ── waveform → metrics ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef _load_dat(path):\n    ts, vs, isup = [], [], []\n    with open(path) as f:\n        for ln in f:\n            p = ln.split()\n            if len(p) < 4 or ln[0] in \"#*@\":\n                continue\n            try:\n                ts.append(float(p[0])); vs.append(float(p[1]))\n                isup.append(float(p[3]))\n            except ValueError:\n                pass\n    return ts, vs, isup\n\n\ndef _interp(x0, y0, x1, y1, y):\n    return x0 + (y - y0) * (x1 - x0) / (y1 - y0)\n\n\ndef osc_metrics_from_dat(dat_path):\n    ts, vs, isup = _load_dat(dat_path)\n    n = len(ts)\n    if n < 50:\n        return {}\n    i0 = int(n * 0.6)\n    hi, lo = max(vs[i0:]), min(vs[i0:])\n    mid, amp = (hi + lo) / 2.0, (hi - lo) / 2.0\n    if amp < 0.5 or hi <= lo:\n        return {}\n\n    t_start = None\n    for i in range(n):\n        if abs(vs[i] - mid) >= 0.8 * amp:\n            t_start = ts[i]\n            break\n    if t_start is None:\n        return {\"startup_time_s\": float(\"inf\"), \"freq_hz\": 0.0,\n                \"duty_cycle_pct\": 0.0, \"volt_high_v\": hi, \"volt_low_v\": lo,\n                \"i_avg_a\": 0.0, \"cycles_sustained\": 0}\n\n    j = 0\n    while j < n and ts[j] < t_start + 3e-9:\n        j += 1\n    rises, falls = [], []\n    above = vs[j] >= mid\n    for k in range(j + 1, n):\n        now = vs[k] >= mid\n        if now != above:\n            tc = _interp(ts[k - 1], vs[k - 1], ts[k], vs[k], mid)\n            (rises if now else falls).append(tc)\n        above = now\n    if len(rises) < 3:\n        return {\"startup_time_s\": t_start, \"freq_hz\": 0.0,\n                \"duty_cycle_pct\": 0.0, \"volt_high_v\": hi, \"volt_low_v\": lo,\n                \"i_avg_a\": 0.0, \"cycles_sustained\": 0}\n\n    freq = (len(rises) - 1) / (rises[-1] - rises[0])\n    duties, fi = [], 0\n    for r0, r1 in zip(rises, rises[1:]):\n        while fi < len(falls) and falls[fi] < r0:\n            fi += 1\n        if fi < len(falls) and falls[fi] < r1:\n            duties.append((falls[fi] - r0) / (r1 - r0))\n    duty = sum(duties) / len(duties) * 100.0 if duties else float(\"nan\")\n\n    w0 = next(i for i in range(n) if ts[i] >= rises[0])\n    idx = range(w0, n)\n    voh = max(vs[i] for i in idx)\n    vol = min(vs[i] for i in idx)\n    i_avg = sum(abs(isup[i]) for i in idx) / len(idx)\n    return {\"startup_time_s\": t_start, \"freq_hz\": freq,\n            \"duty_cycle_pct\": duty, \"volt_high_v\": voh, \"volt_low_v\": vol,\n            \"i_avg_a\": i_avg, \"cycles_sustained\": float(len(rises) - 1)}\n\n\ndef simulate_osc_netlist(netlist: str, cell: str, spec_names,\n                         out_node=None, in_node=None, supplies=None,\n                         bias=None, workdir=None, corner=\"typical\",\n                         temp=27, vdd=3.3, stop=\"12.5u\", step=\"100p\"):\n    from mbg.simulation import run_spice\n    wd = workdir or os.path.join(_HERE, \"simwork\")\n    os.makedirs(wd, exist_ok=True)\n    tag = re.sub(r\"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\", \"_\", f\"{cell}_{corner}_{vdd}V_{temp}C\")\n    wd = os.path.join(wd, tag)\n    os.makedirs(wd, exist_ok=True)\n    deck = build_deck(netlist, cell, vdd=vdd, temp=temp, corner=corner,\n                      stop=stop, step=step)\n    r = run_spice(deck, workdir=wd, fmt=\"dat\", timeout=1200)\n    dats = [p for p in r[\"dat_paths\"] if os.path.basename(p) == \"osc.dat\"]\n    if not dats:\n        raise RuntimeError(\n            f\"ngspice produced no waveform data (rc={r['returncode']}) \"\n            f\"— see {wd}\")\n    return osc_metrics_from_dat(dats[0])\n\n\n# ── sizing knobs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n_DEV_L = re.compile(\n    r\"^(?P<head>\\s*X\\w+\\s+(?:\\S+\\s+){3,4}\\S*(?:fet|FET)\\S*\\s+.*?)\"\n    r\"\\bL\\s*=\\s*(?P<l>[0-9.eE+-]+)(?P<unit>[a-zA-Z]*)(?P<tail>.*)$\")\n_DEV_WP = re.compile(\n    r\"^(?P<head>\\s*X\\w+\\s+(?:\\S+\\s+){3,4}(?P<model>\\S*p\\S*FET\\S*|\\S*pfet\\S*)\\s+.*?)\"\n    r\"\\bW\\s*=\\s*(?P<w>[0-9.eE+-]+)(?P<unit>[a-zA-Z]*)(?P<tail>.*)$\", re.I)\n\n\ndef scale_device_lengths(netlist: str, factor: float,\n                         l_max: float = 10.0) -> str:\n    out = []\n    for line in netlist.splitlines():\n        m = _DEV_L.match(line)\n        if not m:\n            out.append(line)\n            continue\n        unit = m.group(\"unit\") or \"\"\n        l = float(m.group(\"l\"))\n        new_l = l * factor\n        if unit.lower().startswith(\"u\") and new_l >= l_max:\n            new_l = l_max - 0.05\n        out.append(f\"{m.group('head')}L={new_l:g}{unit}{m.group('tail')}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(out) + (\"\\n\" if netlist.endswith(\"\\n\") else \"\")\n\n\ndef scale_pmos_widths(netlist: str, factor: float,\n                      w_max: float = 10.0) -> str:\n    out = []\n    for line in netlist.splitlines():\n        m = _DEV_WP.match(line)\n        if not m:\n            out.append(line)\n            continue\n        unit = m.group(\"unit\") or \"\"\n        w = float(m.group(\"w\"))\n        new_w = w * factor\n        if unit.lower().startswith(\"u\") and new_w >= w_max:\n            new_w = w_max - 0.01\n        out.append(f\"{m.group('head')}W={new_w:g}{unit}{m.group('tail')}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(out) + (\"\\n\" if netlist.endswith(\"\\n\") else \"\")\n",

Alternative deterministic approach WITHOUT extra static path: **power-up ramp in the acceptance testbench**. During VDD ramp, the quasi-static equilibrium tracks; lag seeds mV-scale imbalance deterministically (physics, not numerics). Earlier ramp attempts crawled due to SLOW LIB, not physics. With flat lib, a ramped-supply run costs the same as fixed. This models "normal power-up" faithfully and applies equally to any netlist. AND the spec explicitly says "start automatically from normal power-up" — a supply ramp IS normal power-up!

With 100ns ramp, post-ramp δ≈mV → full swing within ns after ramp. Deterministic enough? Hmm, but if the pinning was exact (LTE keeping system ON the moving quasi-static branch), post-ramp the state could STILL sit at the (now fixed) metastable point with only numerical noise to depart... The ramp helps because tracking lag creates REAL finite offsets (~mV) — those are physical state, not roundoff: departure guaranteed mathematically. ## Assistant (Build · Ox Alpha Free (Unlimited) · 49.8s)

_Thinking:_

CRITICAL DISCOVERY. The flow's PEX simulation used a DIFFERENT extracted file than the one I tested! Look at the flow deck:

```
.option scale=5n
.subckt oscillator OSC_OUT VSS VDD      <- PORT ORDER REVERSED!
X0 ... w=400 l=440                       <- SCALED units (w=400 → 400×5n=2µ ✓)
C0..C33 parasitic caps
```

The flow's PEX netlist (produced by iic-pex.sh, "with m=2 and s=1") is the **scaled** variant: `.option scale=5n` with dimensions in scaled units (w=400 → 2µm), port order `OSC_OUT VSS VDD`, plus real parasitic capacitances.

Why did it not oscillate? **PORT ORDER**: `.subckt oscillator OSC_OUT VSS VDD` — but my deck instantiates `Xdut VDD VSS OSC_OUT oscillator` (assumed VDD VSS OSC_OUT order)! With the actual order (OSC_OUT VSS VDD), my instance connects:
- node VDD(net) → subckt port OSC_OUT
- node VSS(net) → port VSS
- node OSC_OUT(net) → port VDD

So the DUT's internal "VDD" got tied to the probe net and its internal ring output became the supply!! The circuit then = ring powered between... total garbage → stuck state with 229µA. THAT's the flat 3.028V!

Also the earlier `oscillator_extracted_flat.spice` (which I tested standalone, works) has ports `VDD VSS OSC_OUT` — that's a DIFFERENT artifact (the pipeline's own flattened extraction for LVS?). The flow's simulate_pex reads `layout.pex_netlist` = pex["pex_path"] — which pointed to the iic-pex.sh-produced scaled netlist with reordered ports.

FIX in osc_sim.simulate_osc_netlist: detect the actual `.subckt oscillator <ports>` ORDER from the netlist text and instantiate Xdut with nodes mapped accordingly: build port→net mapping where electrical roles are identified by name (case-insensitive): vdd→VDD net, vss→VSS net, osc_out→OSC_OUT net. Since top-level nets I control are literally named VDD/VSS/OSC_OUT, just reorder instance nodes to match subckt declaration.

Implement: regex `^\.subckt\s+\w+\s+(.*)$` → ports list; then instance line: for each declared port p, map name.lower(): 'vdd'→'VDD', 'vss'→'VSS', contains 'osc'→'OSC_OUT'.

ALSO note `.option scale=5n` + w=400/l=440: ngspice scale option scales LENGTH-type params — kept as-is from netlist core (I don't strip .option) ✓ correct handling by design.

AND the parasitic caps reference node names like `a_5656_428#` — fine.

Note the "; **FLOATING" comments on C26-C33 (caps to VSS marked floating?) — they're comments after ';' so harmless; caps still connect... actually `; **FLOATING` is an inline comment; the cap line remains active.

Fix osc_sim.py: add port-order detection in simulate_osc_netlist/build_deck:

```python
def _subckt_ports(netlist, cell):
    m = re.search(rf"^\.subckt\s+{re.escape(cell)}\s+(.+)$", netlist, re.M|re.I)
    return [p for p in m.group(1).split() if "=" not in p] if m else ["VDD","VSS","OSC_OUT"]

def _map_port(p):
    lp = p.lower()
    if lp == "vdd": return "VDD"
    if lp == "vss": return "VSS"
    if "osc" in lp: return "OSC_OUT"
    raise ValueError(f"unexpected port {p}")
```
build_deck needs netlist param to detect — it already receives core_netlist ✓ compute inside.

Then rerun flow. Also the flow archived iteration under results/ox_alpha_oscillator/iterations/run/ — rerun will overwrite/reuse? make_hooks builds into iterations/<tag> per candidate tag; fresh run recreates. Fine.

Also delete stale simwork dirs to avoid confusion (tag collision pre vs pex — now both sims write same dir sequentially; each run overwrites osc.dat before parse... wait run_spice writes input.spice then runs; osc.dat overwritten by wrdata. But if PEX sim FAILS, stale osc.dat from pre sim could be misread as PEX result!!! Add safety: remove existing osc.dat before running in simulate_osc_netlist. ALSO differentiate workdir by source kind: pass distinct subdir via kw? _measure can't tell pre/pex apart... simplest: unlink dat first.)

Patch osc_sim.py now.

Root cause found: the PEX netlist declares ports as `.subckt oscillator OSC_OUT VSS VDD` (different order) while my harness instantiated `Xdut VDD VSS OSC_OUT`. The DUT got cross-connected — hence the stuck state. Fixing with explicit port-order detection, plus removing stale waveforms before each sim:

  "filePath": "/home/huda/opensource-project/Microelectronic-Block-Generator/results/ox_alpha_oscillator/osc_sim.py",

{
  "content": "\"\"\"PVT characterization of the final oscillator (schematic + PEX netlists).\n\nGrid: corner x VDD x TEMP as requested by the design prompt.\nWrites pvt_summary.json + pvt_summary.md incrementally.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\n\nHERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nREPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(HERE))\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(REPO, \"src\"))\nsys.path.insert(0, HERE)\n\nfrom osc_sim import simulate_osc_netlist  # noqa: E402\n\nSCH = os.path.join(HERE, \"generated_netlist.spice\")\nPEX = os.path.join(HERE, \"final\", \"oscillator.pex.spice\")\n\nCORNERS = [\"typical\", \"ss\", \"ff\", \"sf\", \"fs\"]\nVDDS = [3.0, 3.3, 3.6]\nTEMPS = [-40, 27, 125]\n\nSPEC = dict(freq=(10e6, 100e6), duty=(40.0, 60.0), voh=(2.97, None),\n            vol=(None, 0.33), iavg=(None, 1e-3))\n\n\ndef evaluate(m):\n    flags = []\n    f = m.get(\"freq_hz\")\n    if not m:\n        return [\"NO_DATA\"]\n    if f is None or not (10e6 <= f <= 100e6):\n        flags.append(\"FREQ\")\n    d = m.get(\"duty_cycle_pct\")\n    if d is None or not (40.0 <= d <= 60.0):\n        flags.append(\"DUTY\")\n    if (m.get(\"volt_high_v\") or 0) < 2.97:\n        flags.append(\"VOH\")\n    if (m.get(\"volt_low_v\") or 1) > 0.33:\n        flags.append(\"VOL\")\n    if (m.get(\"i_avg_a\") or 1) > 1e-3:\n        flags.append(\"IAVG\")\n    if (m.get(\"cycles_sustained\") or 0) < 100:\n        flags.append(\"CYCLES\")\n    if (m.get(\"startup_time_s\") or 1) > 5e-6:\n        flags.append(\"TSTART\")\n    return flags\n\n\ndef main():\n    results = []\n    out_json = os.path.join(HERE, \"pvt_summary.json\")\n    total = len(CORNERS) * len(VDDS) * len(TEMPS) * 2\n    done = 0\n    t00 = time.time()\n    for src_name, path in ((\"sch\", SCH), (\"pex\", PEX)):\n        netlist = open(path).read()\n        for corner in CORNERS:\n            for vdd in VDDS:\n                for temp in TEMPS:\n                    t0 = time.time()\n                    try:\n                        m = simulate_osc_netlist(netlist, \"oscillator\", [],\n                                                 corner=corner, temp=temp,\n                                                 vdd=vdd)\n                        err = \"\"\n                    except Exception as e:      # noqa: BLE001\n                        m, err = {}, str(e)[:200]\n                    row = {\"netlist\": src_name, \"corner\": corner,\n                           \"vdd\": vdd, \"temp\": temp,\n                           \"metrics\": m, \"fail_flags\": evaluate(m),\n                           \"wall_s\": round(time.time() - t0, 1), \"err\": err}\n                    results.append(row)\n                    done += 1\n                    ok = \"PASS\" if not row[\"fail_flags\"] else \",\".join(\n                        row[\"fail_flags\"])\n                    print(f\"[{done}/{total}] {src_name} {corner:>7} \"\n                          f\"{vdd:.1f}V {temp:>4}C -> {ok} \"\n                          f\"f={m.get('freq_hz', 0)/1e6:7.2f}MHz \"\n                          f\"duty={m.get('duty_cycle_pct', 0):5.2f}% \"\n                          f\"({row['wall_s']}s)\", flush=True)\n                    with open(out_json, \"w\") as f:\n                        json.dump(results, f, indent=1)\n    print(f\"total wall: {round(time.time()-t00, 1)}s\")\n    npass = sum(1 for r in results if r[\"netlist\"] == \"pex\"\n                and not r[\"fail_flags\"])\n    print(f\"PEX points passing all specs: {npass}/{len(CORNERS)*len(VDDS)*len(TEMPS)}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n",
  "filePath": "/home/huda/opensource-project/Microelectronic-Block-Generator/results/ox_alpha_oscillator/pvt_run.py"
}

```text
Design a **self-starting oscillator** using the Microelectronic-Block-Generator framework with the **GF180MCU `gf180mcuD` PDK**.

{
  "content": "\"\"\"Render pvt_summary.json -> pvt_summary.md tables.\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport os\n\nHERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nrows = json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, \"pvt_summary.json\")))\n\nCORNERS = [\"typical\", \"ss\", \"ff\", \"sf\", \"fs\"]\nVDDS = [3.0, 3.3, 3.6]\nTEMPS = [-40, 27, 125]\n\n\ndef cell(rows, corner, vdd, temp, key):\n    for r in rows:\n        if (r[\"corner\"] == corner and r[\"vdd\"] == vdd and r[\"temp\"] == temp\n                and r[\"netlist\"] == key):\n            m = r[\"metrics\"]\n            if not m:\n                return r[\"fail_flags\"] or [\"NO DATA\"]\n            f = [x for x in r[\"fail_flags\"]]\n            return (f\"{m.get('freq_hz', 0)/1e6:.1f}MHz \"\n                    f\"d{m.get('duty_cycle_pct', 0):.1f}%\"\n                    + (\" *\" + \",\".join(f) if f else \"\"))\n    return \"MISSING\"\n\n\nlines = [\n    \"# PVT Characterization — self-starting ring oscillator (ox-alpha)\",\n    \"\",\n    \"Final design, nominal pre-layout `f=53.8 MHz`; extracted (PEX) \"\n    \"`f=45.3 MHz`. Each cell: frequency, duty cycle; `*FLAG` marks a \"\n    \"specification miss (window: 10-100 MHz, duty 40-60%, VOH>2.97 V, \"\n    \"VOL<0.33 V, Iavg<1 mA, >=100 cycles, startup<5 us).\",\n    \"\",\n]\nfor key, title in ((\"sch\", \"Pre-layout (schematic) netlist\"),\n                   (\"pex\", \"Extracted (PEX) netlist\")):\n    lines += [f\"## {title}\", \"\",\n              \"| corner | \" + \" | \".join(\n                  f\"{v} V, {t} °C\" for v in VDDS for t in TEMPS) + \" |\",\n              \"|\" + \"---|\" * (1 + len(VDDS) * len(TEMPS))]\n    for c in CORNERS:\n        cells = [cell(rows, c, v, t, key) for v in VDDS for t in TEMPS]\n        lines.append(f\"| {c} | \" + \" | \".join(cells) + \" |\")\n    lines.append(\"\")\n\nnpex = sum(1 for r in rows if r[\"netlist\"] == \"pex\" and not r[\"fail_flags\"])\nnsch = sum(1 for r in rows if r[\"netlist\"] == \"sch\" and not r[\"fail_flags\"])\ntot = len(VDDS) * len(TEMPS) * len(CORNERS)\nfreqs = sorted(r[\"metrics\"][\"freq_hz\"] / 1e6 for r in rows\n               if r[\"netlist\"] == \"pex\" and r[\"metrics\"].get(\"freq_hz\"))\nlines += [\n    f\"**Result:** schematic {nsch}/{tot} points pass all specs; extracted \"\n    f\"{npex}/{tot} points pass.\",\n    f\"PEX frequency range across the full grid: {freqs[0]:.1f} - \"\n    f\"{freqs[-1]:.1f} MHz.\" if freqs else \"\",\n    \"\",\n    \"Simulation model: flattened GF180MCU ngspice parameter library \"\n    \"(value-identical, see pdk_flat/); ideal DC power-up, no stimulus, \"\n    \"no forced initial conditions anywhere.\",\n]\n\nout = os.path.join(HERE, \"pvt_summary.md\")\nopen(out, \"w\").write(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\")\nprint(\"wrote\", out)\n",

  "content": "\"\"\"One-shot PVT point: args corner vdd temp netlist(sch|pex). Appends to JSON.\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\n\nHERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nREPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(HERE))\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(REPO, \"src\"))\nsys.path.insert(0, HERE)\n\nfrom osc_sim import simulate_osc_netlist  # noqa: E402\nfrom pvt_run import evaluate  # noqa: E402\n\ncorner, vdd, temp, src = (sys.argv[1], float(sys.argv[2]), int(sys.argv[3]),\n                          sys.argv[4])\npath = os.path.join(HERE, \"generated_netlist.spice\" if src == \"sch\"\n                    else \"final\", \"oscillator.pex.spice\")\nif src == \"sch\":\n    path = os.path.join(HERE, \"generated_netlist.spice\")\nnetlist = open(path).read()\nt0 = __import__(\"time\").time()\ntry:\n    m = simulate_osc_netlist(netlist, \"oscillator\", [], corner=corner,\n                             temp=temp, vdd=vdd)\n    err = \"\"\nexcept Exception as e:                                  # noqa: BLE001\n    m, err = {}, str(e)[:200]\nrow = {\"netlist\": src, \"corner\": corner, \"vdd\": vdd, \"temp\": temp,\n       \"metrics\": m, \"fail_flags\": evaluate(m),\n       \"wall_s\": round(__import__(\"time\").time() - t0, 1), \"err\": err}\n\nout_json = os.path.join(HERE, \"pvt_summary.json\")\nrows = json.load(open(out_json)) if os.path.exists(out_json) else []\nrows = [r for r in rows if not (r[\"netlist\"] == src and r[\"corner\"] == corner\n                                and r[\"vdd\"] == vdd and r[\"temp\"] == temp)]\nrows.append(row)\njson.dump(rows, open(out_json, \"w\"), indent=1)\nok = \"PASS\" if not row[\"fail_flags\"] else \",\".join(row[\"fail_flags\"])\nprint(f\"{src} {corner} {vdd}V {temp}C -> {ok} \"\n      f\"f={m.get('freq_hz', 0)/1e6:.2f}MHz duty={m.get('duty_cycle_pct', 0):.2f}%\")\n",

# `/mbg-full-auto` — Self-Starting Ring Oscillator (gf180mcuD) — **TAPEOUT_READY**

**Status: `SUCCESS` · tapeout_ready: true** — every gate condition PASS (run 2; run 1 hit a harness `TOOL_FAILURE`, diagnosed and fixed — circuit unchanged).

## Design

9-stage CMOS ring oscillator, single-ended, no output buffer — **18 MOS / 11 nets**, only `nfet_03v3`/`pfet_03v3`, ports exactly `VDD VSS OSC_OUT`. Sizing L=2.2 µm, Wn=2 µm, Wp=4 µm. Self-start comes from a deliberate stage-1 PMOS asymmetry (3.8 µ vs 4.0 µ): oscillation establishes **~16 ns after ideal DC power-up** — no stimulus pin, no `.ic`/`.nodeset`/`uic` anywhere.

## Measured results (12.5 µs transient, ≥560 cycles)

| metric | target | pre-layout | **PEX (final)** |
|---|---|---|---|
| frequency | 10–100 MHz | 53.82 MHz | **45.33 MHz** |
| startup | < 5 µs | 15.7 ns | **4.2 ns** |
| duty cycle | 40–60 % | 48.81 % | **48.68 %** |
| OSC_OUT high | > 2.97 V | 3.577 V | **3.527 V** |
| OSC_OUT low | < 0.33 V | −0.251 V | **−0.208 V** |
| avg supply current | < 1 mA | 103.6 µA | **104.4 µA** |
| sustained cycles | ≥ 100 | 671 | **565** |

VOH/VOL include fast-edge coupling overshoot beyond the rails (~7 %); within spec bounds, noted as a characteristic.

## PVT (supplementary characterization — not a gate condition)

**90/90 points pass all specs**: {tt, ss, ff, sf, fs} × VDD {3.0, 3.3, 3.6 V} × TEMP {−40, 27, 125 °C}, on both schematic and extracted netlists. Extracted range: **27.6–69.3 MHz**, duty 48.1–49.3 %. Worst case: ss/3.0 V/125 °C at 27.6 MHz — >2.7× margin above 10 MHz.

## Honest engineering notes

- **Flattened model library (`pdk_flat/`)**: the stock GF180MCU ngspice file re-interprets ~1000 quoted parameter expressions *per Newton iteration* (transients took hours). I pre-evaluated them under nominal switches; verified equivalent by identical operating points and a **bit-identical 20 ns device transient** vs the original library.
- First flow run failed PEX simulation: Magic's PEX netlist declares ports `OSC_OUT VSS VDD`; my harness assumed schematic order and cross-connected the DUT. Fixed by port-order detection (`osc_sim.py`) — a harness bug, not a design bug.
- Reviews ran at all stages (Devil: INFO-only findings; Angel: no changes needed). Monte Carlo was not requested and did not run — no claim made about it.
